The explicit forms of the collision integrals are then derived once the off-diagonal density matrix elements are known, which are presented in the next section.

1.8
Off-diagonal density matrix

Inserting the various driving terms into the formal solution of the off-diagonal density matrix, one finds

\displaystyle S_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)ab}\left[D_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\right]=e\mathbf{E}\cdot\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime ab}\frac{f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}-f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{b}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}},

(1.74a)

\displaystyle S_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)ab}\left[I_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\right]=i\pi\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ac}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cb}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\left[\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-1)}\right)\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c})+\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}b}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-1)}\right)\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c})\right],

(1.74b)

at linear order in the electric field. And, similarly, for the analogous quadratic-response counterparts, we obtain

\displaystyle S_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(-1)ab}\left[D_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(-1)}\right]=e\mathbf{E}\cdot\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime ab}\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}b}^{(-1)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}},

(1.75a)

\displaystyle S_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(-1)ab}\left[I_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(-1)}\right]=i\pi\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ac}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cb}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\left[(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c})+(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}b}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c})\right],

(1.75b)

as well as

\displaystyle S_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(0)ab}\left[D_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\right]=e\mathbf{E}\cdot\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime ab}\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(0)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}b}^{(0)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}},

(1.76a)

\displaystyle S_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(0)ab}\left[I_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\right]=i\pi\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ac}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cb}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\left[(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-1)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c})+(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}b}^{(-1)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-1)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c})\right].

(1.76b)

The contribution from

I_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{\prime(0)}
is obtained by inserting Eq. (
1.71b
) into the relevant term in Eq. (
1.18
), which yields

\begin{split}S_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(0)ab}\left[I_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{\prime(0)}\right]&=-\frac{e}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\mathbf{E}\cdot\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}+i\delta}\Biggl\{\Braket{iU_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ac}(\bm{\partial}_{\mathbf{k}}+\bm{\partial}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}})\left(U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cb}\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}b}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-1)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}+i\delta}\right)}\\
&+\sum_{d}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ac}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{db}}\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{cd}\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}b}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}d}^{(-1)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{d}+i\delta}-\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ac}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cd}}\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}}^{db}\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}d}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-1)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{d}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}+i\delta}\right]\Biggr\}\\
&-\frac{e}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\mathbf{E}\cdot\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}+i\delta}\Biggl\{\Braket{iU_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cb}(\bm{\partial}_{\mathbf{k}}+\bm{\partial}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}})\left(U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ac}\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-1)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}+i\delta}\right)}\\
&+\sum_{d}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{dc}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cb}}\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}}^{ad}\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}d}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-1)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{d}+i\delta}-\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ad}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cb}}\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{dc}\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}d}^{(-1)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{d}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}+i\delta}\right]\Biggr\}.\end{split}

(1.77)

The find the remaining contributions from
D_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{\prime(0)}
and
I_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{\prime\prime(0)}
, recalling Eq. (
1.19
), we arrive at the following terms

\begin{split}S_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(0)ab}\left[D_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{\prime(0)}[D_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}]\right]=&-\frac{ie\mathbf{E}\cdot\bm{\partial}_{\mathbf{k}}S_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)ab}\left[D_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\right]}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}-\frac{e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\Big\{\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{\prime ab}(\mathcal{A}_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\mathcal{A}_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{b})\frac{f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}-f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{b}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\\
&-\sum_{c}\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{\prime ac}\mathcal{A}_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{\prime cb}\left(\frac{f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}-f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{c}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}-\frac{f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{b}-f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{c}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\right)\Bigg\},\end{split}

(1.78)

\begin{split}S_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(0)ab}&\left[D_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{\prime(0)}[I_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}]\right]=-\frac{ie\mathbf{E}\cdot}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\Big\{\bm{\partial}_{\mathbf{k}}S_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)ab}\left[I_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\right]\\
&+\pi\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}cd}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}}^{ac}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{cd}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{db}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\left[(n_{E\mathbf{k}b}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}d}^{(-1)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{d})+(n_{E\mathbf{k}c}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}d}^{(-1)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{d})\right]_{c\neq b}\\
&-\pi\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}cd}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}}^{cb}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ad}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{dc}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\left[(n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}d}^{(-1)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{d})+(n_{E\mathbf{k}c}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}d}^{(-1)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{d})\right]_{c\neq a}\Big\},\end{split}

(1.79)

\begin{split}S_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(0)ab}&\left[I_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{\prime\prime(0)}[D_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(-1)}]\right]=i\pi e\mathbf{E}\cdot\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}cd}\\
&\times\Bigg\{\left[\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ac}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cd}}\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime db}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}d}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}b}^{(-1)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{d}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}-\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ac}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{db}}\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\prime cd}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}d}^{(-1)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{d}}\right]\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c})\\
&-\left[\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ad}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cb}}\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\prime dc}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}d}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-1)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{d}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}-\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{dc}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cb}}\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}}^{\prime ad}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}d}^{(-1)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{d}}\right]\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c})\bigg\},\end{split}

(1.80)

\begin{split}&S_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(0)ab}\left[I_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{\prime\prime(0)}[I_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(-1)}]\right]=-\frac{\pi^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}cde}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c})\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ac}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cd}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{de}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{eb}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{d}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\left[(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}b}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}e}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{e})+(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}d}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}e}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{d}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{e})\right]_{d\neq b}\\
&-\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ac}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{db}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{ce}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ed}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{d}}\left[(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}d}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}e}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{d}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{e})+(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}e}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{e})\right]_{d\neq c}\Big\}\\
&-\frac{\pi^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{b}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}cde}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c})\\
&\times\bigg\{\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{dc}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cb}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{ae}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ed}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{d}}\left[(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}d}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}e}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{d}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{e})+(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}e}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{e})\right]_{d\neq a}\\
&-\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ad}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{cb}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{de}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ec}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{d}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}\left[(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}e}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{e})+(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}d}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}e}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{d}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{e})\right]_{d\neq c}\Big\}.\end{split}

(1.81)

Thus, all the off-diagonal density matrix elements are expressed now in terms of diagonal terms. When inserted into the various collision integrals, the carrier densities are derived, the forms of which are presented in the next section.

1.9
\mathcal{I}_{1}
and
\mathcal{I}_{2}
in Eq. (
1.23
)

\mathcal{I}_{1}=J_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}\left(S_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\left[D_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{\prime(0)}[I_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}]\right]\right)+J_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}\left(S_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\left[I_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{\prime(0)}\right]\right)+J_{E\mathbf{k}}^{a}\left(S_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\left[I_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\right]\right)+J_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}\left(S_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\left[I_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{\prime\prime(0)}[D_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(-1)}]\right]\right),

(1.82)

\mathcal{I}_{2}=J_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}\left(S_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\left[D_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{\prime(0)}[D_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}]\right]\right)+J_{E\mathbf{k}}^{a}\left(S_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\left[D_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(0)}\right]\right)+J_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}}^{a}\left(f_{0\mathbf{k}}\right).

(1.83)

1.10
Carrier densities

1.10.1
Linear-response densities

The linear-response carrier densities are obtained by solving Eq. (
1.13
). As outlined above, this yields
[
33
]

\begin{split}&n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(\text{sk})}=-\frac{2\pi^{2}}{\hbar}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}bcd}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\\
&\times\text{Im}\Biggl\{\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{cd}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{da}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\left[\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-1)}\right)\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})+\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}c}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-1)}\right)\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})\right]_{c\neq a}\\
&+\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ca}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{bd}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{dc}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}\left[\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-1)}\right)\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})+\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-1)}\right)\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})\right]_{c\neq b}\biggr\},\end{split}

(1.84)

and

n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(\text{sj})}=-\frac{2\pi}{\hbar}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}}e\mathbf{E}\cdot\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\Bigl\{\text{Im}\Braket{\left[\left(\bm{\partial}_{\mathbf{k}}+\bm{\partial}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\right)U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\right]U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}}-\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}}\left(\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\Bigr\}.

(1.85)

The various quadratic-response carrier densities are considerably more numerous. However, they are evaluated in a fairly similar manner, the explicit forms of which are presented below.

1.10.2
Mixed scattering

The contribution from
n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{\text{(sj,o)}}
is expressed as

n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{\text{(sj,o)}}=\mathcal{N}^{(\text{sj,o})}_{1,\mathbf{k}a}+\mathcal{N}^{(\text{sj,o})}_{2,\mathbf{k}a},

(1.86)

with

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{sj,o})}_{1,\mathbf{k}a}=&-\frac{2\pi}{\hbar^{2}}eE^{\mu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\rho}\left[\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\text{Im}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\left[\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\mu}\right)U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}\right]}\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}^{(-1)}\right)\right]\\
&-\frac{2\pi}{\hbar^{2}}eE^{\mu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\rho}\left[\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}}\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}^{(-1)}\right)\right],\end{split}

(1.87)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{sj,o})}_{2,\mathbf{k}a}=&-\frac{4\pi}{\hbar}eE^{\mu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\\
&\times\left(\text{Re}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{\prime ca}\right]\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}^{(-1)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}+\text{Re}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ca}}\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\prime bc}\right]\frac{n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}^{(-1)}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}\right),\end{split}

(1.88)

where the velocity factors here and elsewhere arise from application of Eq. (
1.73
). The remaining mixed-scattering terms read

\begin{split}n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{(\text{sk,o})}=&-\frac{2\pi^{2}}{\hbar}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}bcd}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\Biggl\{\frac{\text{Im}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{cd}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{da}}\right]}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\\
&\times\left[(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})+(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}c}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})\right]_{c\neq a}\\
&+\frac{\text{Im}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ca}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{bd}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{dc}}\right]}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}\\
&\times\left[(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})+(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})\right]_{c\neq b}\biggr\},\end{split}

(1.89)

\displaystyle n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{(\text{o,sj})}

\displaystyle=\frac{e}{\hbar}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\mathbf{E}\cdot\bm{\partial}_{\mathbf{k}}n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(\text{sj})},

(1.90a)

\displaystyle n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{(\text{o,sk})}

\displaystyle=\frac{e}{\hbar}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\mathbf{E}\cdot\bm{\partial}_{\mathbf{k}}n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(\text{sk})}.

(1.90b)

1.10.3
Secondary side jump

The secondary side jump density is given by

n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{\text{(ssj)}}=\mathcal{N}^{(\text{ssj})}_{1,\mathbf{k}a}+\mathcal{N}^{(\text{ssj})}_{2,\mathbf{k}a},

(1.91)

where

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{ssj})}_{1,\mathbf{k}a}=&-\frac{2\pi}{\hbar^{2}}eE^{\mu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\rho}\left[\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\text{Im}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\left[\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\mu}\right)U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}\right]}\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(0)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}^{(0)}\right)\right]\\
&-\frac{2\pi}{\hbar^{2}}eE^{\mu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\rho}\left[\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}}\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(0)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}^{(0)}\right)\right],\end{split}

(1.92)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{ssj})}_{2,\mathbf{k}a}=&-\frac{2\pi}{\hbar}eE^{\mu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\frac{\text{Re}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{\prime ca}\right]}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\\
&\times\left[\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(0)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}^{(0)}\right)\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})-\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}c}^{(0)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}^{(0)}\right)\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\right]\\
&-\frac{2\pi}{\hbar}eE^{\mu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\frac{\text{Re}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ca}}\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\prime bc}\right]}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}\\
&\times\left[\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(0)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}^{(0)}\right)\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})-\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(0)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(0)}\right)\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c})\right].\end{split}

(1.93)

1.10.4
Tertiary skew scattering

Similar to the previous skew scattering expressions, the tertiary skew scattering density reads

\begin{split}n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{(\text{tsk})}=&-\frac{2\pi^{2}}{\hbar}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}bcd}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\Biggl\{\frac{\text{Im}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{cd}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{da}}\right]}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\\
&\times\left[(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-1)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})+(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}c}^{(-1)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-1)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})\right]_{c\neq a}\\
&+\frac{\text{Im}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ca}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{bd}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{dc}}\right]}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}\\
&\times\left[(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}^{(-1)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-1)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})+(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}c}^{(-1)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-1)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})\right]_{c\neq b}\biggr\}.\end{split}

(1.94)

1.10.5
Quadratic side jump

The quadratic side jump density is rather lengthy, as it is comprised of the three collision integrals given by Eq. (
1.26
). After a number of cancellations, the remaining terms are expressed as

n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{(\text{qsj})}=\sum_{i=1}^{8}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{qsj})}_{i,\mathbf{k}a},

(1.95)

where

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{qsj})}_{1,\mathbf{k}a}=&-\frac{\pi}{4}\hbar e^{2}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\partial^{4}f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a})^{4}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}}\left[\mathbf{E}\cdot\left(\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}+\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\right]^{2}\\
&-\pi\hbar e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\partial^{3}f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a})^{4}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\biggl\{\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\rho}\left[\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}}v_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}\left(v_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{a}+v_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\right]\\
&+\frac{1}{3}\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mu}v_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{a}+2\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\mu}v_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}}+\left(v_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}+v_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\text{Re}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\left[\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\nu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\nu}\right)U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}\right]}\biggr\},\end{split}

(1.96)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{qsj})}_{2,\mathbf{k}a}=&-\frac{\pi}{\hbar}e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\partial^{2}f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a})^{2}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\\
&\times\biggl\{\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\rho}\left(\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\sigma}\left[\frac{v_{\sigma\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\left(v_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}v_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-v_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}v_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}}\right]\right)\\
&+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\rho}\left[\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mu}v_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{a}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\mu}v_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}}\right]\\
&+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\rho}\left[\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\left(3v_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}+v_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\text{Re}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\left[\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\nu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\nu}\right)U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}\right]}\right]\\
&+\text{Re}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\left[\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\mu}\right)\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\nu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\nu}\right)U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}\right]}\biggr\},\end{split}

(1.97)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{qsj})}_{3,\mathbf{k}a}=&-\frac{\pi}{\hbar^{2}}e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\\
&\times\biggl\{\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\rho}\left(\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\sigma}\left[\frac{v_{\sigma\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\left(v_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-v_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\text{Re}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\left[\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\nu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\nu}\right)U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}\right]}\right]\right)\\
&+2\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\rho}\left(\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\text{Re}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\left[\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\mu}\right)\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\nu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\nu}\right)U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}\right]}\right)\biggr\},\end{split}

(1.98)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{qsj})}_{4,\mathbf{k}a}=&\frac{4\pi}{\hbar^{2}}e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\\
&\times\biggl\{\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\rho}\left[\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\text{Im}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\left[\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\nu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\nu}\right)U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}\right]}\right]\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\rho}\left(\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\left(\mathcal{A}_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\mathcal{A}_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}}\right)\biggr\}\\
&+\frac{2\pi}{\hbar}e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\partial^{2}f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a})^{2}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}b}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\biggl\{\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\text{Im}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\left[\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\nu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\nu}\right)U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}\right]}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\mathcal{A}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\left(\mathcal{A}_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\mathcal{A}_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}}\biggr\},\end{split}

(1.99)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{qsj})}_{5,\mathbf{k}a}=&\pi e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\frac{\partial^{2}f_{0\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\partial(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})^{2}}\frac{\text{Im}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}\mathcal{A}^{\prime ca}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}\right]}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\\
&\times\left[\left(v_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}+v_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})-\left(v_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{c}+v_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\right]\\
&+\frac{2\pi}{\hbar}e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\frac{\text{Im}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\left[\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\nu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\nu}\right)\left(U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}\mathcal{A}^{\prime ca}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}\right)\right]}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\\
&+2\pi e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\frac{\text{Im}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}\mathcal{A}^{\prime ca}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}\right]}{\left(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}\right)^{2}}v_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{c}\left(\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}}-\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{c}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\right)\\
&-\frac{2\pi}{\hbar}e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{c}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\frac{\text{Im}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\left[\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\nu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\nu}\right)\left(U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}\right)\right]}\mathcal{A}^{\prime ca}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}},\end{split}

(1.100)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{qsj})}_{6,\mathbf{k}a}=&\frac{2\pi}{\hbar}e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\frac{\text{Im}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\left[\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\nu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\nu}\right)\left(U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ca}\mathcal{A}^{\prime bc}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\right)\right]}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}\\
&+2\pi e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\frac{\text{Im}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ca}}\mathcal{A}^{\prime bc}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\right]}{\left(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}\right)^{2}}\left[v_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}-\frac{\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\cdot\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\left(v_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-v_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\right]\\
&-2\pi e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}\frac{\text{Im}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ca}}\mathcal{A}^{\prime bc}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\right]}{\left(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}\right)^{2}}\left[v_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}+\frac{\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\cdot\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\left(v_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-v_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\right]\\
&+4\pi e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\text{Im}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ca}}\mathcal{A}^{\prime bc}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\right]\frac{\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\cdot\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}\left(v_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-v_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\frac{f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}-f_{0\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}{\left(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}\right)^{3}}\\
&+\frac{2\pi}{\hbar}e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c})\frac{\text{Im}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}\left[\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\nu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\nu}\right)\left(U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ca}\right)\right]}\mathcal{A}^{\prime bc}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}},\end{split}

(1.101)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{qsj})}_{7,\mathbf{k}a}=&\frac{2\pi}{\hbar}e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\left(\mathcal{A}_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\mathcal{A}_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\frac{\text{Im}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}\mathcal{A}^{\prime ca}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\\
&-\frac{2\pi}{\hbar}e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{c}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\left(\mathcal{A}_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{c}-\mathcal{A}_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\frac{\text{Im}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}\mathcal{A}^{\prime ca}_{\mu\mathbf{k}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\\
&+\frac{4\pi}{\hbar}e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\left(\mathcal{A}_{\nu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\mathcal{A}_{\nu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\right)\frac{\text{Im}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ca}}\mathcal{A}^{\prime bc}_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}},\end{split}

(1.102)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{qsj})}_{8,\mathbf{k}a}=&\frac{2\pi}{\hbar}e^{2}E^{\mu}E^{\nu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}bc}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{ba}}\\
&\times\left[\frac{g_{\mu\nu,\mathbf{k}}^{ac}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\left(\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}}-\frac{f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}-f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{c}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\right)-\frac{g_{\mu\nu,\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{bc}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}\left(\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}}-\frac{f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{a}-f_{0\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{c}}\right)\right].\end{split}

(1.103)

1.10.6
Cubic skew scattering

The density of cubic skew-scattered carriers arises from the lengthy off-diagonal element given by Eq. (
1.81
) and results in the expression

n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{(\text{csk})}=\sum_{i=1}^{8}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{csk})}_{i,\mathbf{k}a},

(1.104)

with

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{csk})}_{1,\mathbf{k}a}=&\frac{2\pi^{3}}{\hbar}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}_{1}bcdef}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}_{1}}^{d})\frac{\text{Re}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}_{1}}^{cd}U_{\mathbf{k}_{1}\mathbf{k}}^{de}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{ef}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{fa}}\right]_{\begin{subarray}{c}c,e\neq a\end{subarray}}}{(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c})(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{e})}\\
&\times\left[(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}f}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{f})+(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}_{1}d}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}f}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}_{1}}^{d}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{f})\right],\end{split}

(1.105)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{csk})}_{2,\mathbf{k}a}=&\frac{2\pi^{3}}{\hbar}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}_{1}bcdef}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}_{1}}^{d})\frac{\text{Re}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}_{1}}^{cd}U_{\mathbf{k}_{1}\mathbf{k}}^{ea}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}_{1}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{df}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}_{1}}^{fe}}\right]_{\begin{subarray}{c}c\neq a\\
e\neq d\end{subarray}}}{(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c})(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}_{1}}^{d}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}_{1}}^{e})}\\
&\times\left[(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}_{1}e}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}f}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}_{1}}^{e}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{f})+(n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}_{1}d}^{(-2)}-n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}f}^{(-2)})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}_{1}}^{d}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{f})\right],\end{split}

1.10.7
Anomalous skew scattering

The carrier density associated with anomalous skew scattering is given by

n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}a}^{(\text{ask})}=\sum_{i=1}^{30}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{ask})}_{i,\mathbf{k}a},

(1.113)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{ask})}_{1,\mathbf{k}a}&=\frac{2\pi^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}eE^{\mu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}bcd}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})\\
&\times\text{Re}\Biggl\{\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{\rho}\left(\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d}\rvert^{2}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{cd}\left(\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{\mu}+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{\mu}\right)\left[U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{da}\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-1)}\right)\right]}\right)\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{\rho}\left(\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d}\rvert^{2}}\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{\sigma}\left[\frac{v_{\sigma\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d}\rvert^{2}}\left(v_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}-v_{\mu\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d}\right)\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{cd}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{da}}\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-1)}\right)\right]\right)\Biggr\}_{\begin{subarray}{c}c\neq a\end{subarray}},\end{split}

(1.114)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{ask})}_{2,\mathbf{k}a}&=\frac{2\pi^{2}}{\hbar^{2}}eE^{\mu}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}bcd}\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b})\delta(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d})\\

&+\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{\rho}\left(\frac{v_{\rho\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{d}\rvert^{2}}\partial_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{\sigma}\left[\frac{v_{\sigma\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}}{\lvert\bm{v}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{b}\rvert^{2}}v_{\mu\mathbf{k}}^{a}\frac{\text{Re}\left[\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}^{ab}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{bc}}\Braket{U_{\mathbf{k}\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}}^{cd}U_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}\mathbf{k}}^{da}}\right]}{\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{a}-\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{c}}\left(n_{E\mathbf{k}a}^{(-1)}-n_{E\mathbf{k}^{\prime\prime}d}^{(-1)}\right)\right]\right)\Biggr\}_{\begin{subarray}{c}c\neq a\end{subarray}},\end{split}

1.11
Carrier densities and conductivities of Dirac fermions

1.11.1
Carrier densities

For the model of Dirac fermions given by Eq. (
1.52
), to first order in tilting, the linear-response carrier densities associated with special scattering are obtained as

n_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(\text{sj})+}=\frac{e}{2\hbar}\frac{n_{I}U_{0}^{2}}{v}E_{x}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{+}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}}\cos\theta\left[\sin\theta\sin\phi+\frac{t_{x}}{4v}\sin^{2}\theta\sin 2\phi-\frac{t_{y}}{4v}(3+\cos 2\theta+\sin^{2}\theta\cos 2\phi)\right],

(1.144)

\begin{split}n_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(\text{sk})+}&=\frac{3e}{64\hbar^{2}}(n_{I}U_{0}^{2})^{2}\frac{h_{k}}{v^{3}}E_{x}(\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{+})^{2}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{+}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}}\sin\theta\sin 2\theta\biggl\{\sin\theta\sin\phi-\frac{t_{x}}{6v}(1+5\cos^{2}\theta)\sin 2\phi\\
&\hskip 137.9979pt-\frac{t_{y}}{v}\left[1+\cos^{2}\theta-\frac{1}{6}(1+5\cos^{2}\theta)\cos 2\phi\right]\biggr\},\end{split}

(1.145)

\begin{split}n_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(\text{sk})-}&=-\frac{3e}{64\hbar^{2}}(n_{I}U_{0}^{2})^{2}\frac{h_{k}}{v^{3}}E_{x}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{-}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{+}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}}\sin\theta\sin 2\theta\biggl\{\sin\theta\sin\phi-\frac{5t_{x}}{6v}(1+\cos^{2}\theta)\sin 2\phi\\
&\hskip 137.9979pt+\frac{t_{y}}{v}\left[\frac{1}{3}-\cos^{2}\theta+\frac{5}{6}(1+\cos^{2}\theta)\cos 2\phi\right]\biggr\},\end{split}

(1.146)

where we note that
n_{E\mathbf{k}}^{(\text{sj})-}=0
when the Fermi level lies in the upper band. Note also that in the limit of vanishing tilting, these reduce to the forms obtained in Ref.
[
33
]
.

Moving on to the mixed scattering densities in the quadratic responses, recalling Eq. (
1.86
), we find

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}^{(\text{sj,o})}_{1,\mathbf{k}+}=&\frac{e^{2}}{4\hbar}\frac{n_{I}U_{0}^{2}}{vh_{k}}E_{x}^{2}\left(\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}\right)^{2}v_{x\mathbf{k}}^{+}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{+}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}}\cos\theta\left[\sin\phi\sin\theta+\frac{t_{x}}{v}\sin 2\phi\sin^{2}\theta\right.\\
&-\left.\frac{t_{y}}{v}\left(\cos 2\theta+\sin^{2}\theta\cos 2\phi\right)\right],\end{split}

(1.147)

and

\mathcal{N}^{(\text{sj,o})}_{2,\mathbf{k}+}=-\frac{e^{2}}{4\hbar}\frac{v^{2}}{h_{k}^{2}}E_{x}^{2}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{+}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}}\frac{\sin 2\theta\sin^{2}\theta}{5+3\cos 2\theta}\left[\frac{t_{x}}{v}\left(3\sin\phi+\sin 3\phi\right)+\frac{t_{y}}{v}\left(3\cos\phi-\cos 3\phi\right)\right].

(1.148)

The skew scattering contribution given by Eq. (
1.89

n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}+}^{(\text{sk,o})}=\mathcal{N}_{1,\mathbf{k}+}^{(\text{sk,o})}+\mathcal{N}_{2,\mathbf{k}+}^{(\text{sk,o})},

(1.149)

where

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}_{1,\mathbf{k}+}^{(\text{sk,o})}=&\frac{e^{2}}{128\hbar}E_{x}^{2}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{+}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}}\frac{v^{2}}{h_{k}^{2}}\frac{\sin 2\theta}{(5+3\cos 2\theta)^{4}}\bigg\{\frac{3t_{x}}{v}\sin\phi(3487+6504\cos 2\theta+2188\cos 4\theta\\
&+88\cos 6\theta+21\cos 8\theta)+\frac{2t_{y}}{v}\cos\phi(9463+9912\cos 2\theta+1100\cos 4\theta+8\cos 6\theta\\
&-3\cos 8\theta)\biggr\},\end{split}

(1.150)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}_{2,\mathbf{k}+}^{(\text{sk,o})}=&\frac{3e^{2}}{8\hbar}E_{x}^{2}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}\frac{\partial^{2}f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{+}}{\partial(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{+})^{2}}\frac{v^{2}}{h_{k}}\frac{\sin^{2}\theta\sin 2\theta}{(5+3\cos 2\theta)^{3}}\bigg\{\frac{t_{x}}{v}\sin\phi(197+148\cos 2\theta+7\cos 4\theta)\\
&+\frac{t_{y}}{v}\cos\phi(29+4\cos 2\theta-\cos 4\theta)\biggr\},\end{split}

(1.151)

and

n_{E^{2}\mathbf{k}-}^{(\text{sk,o})}=\mathcal{N}_{1,\mathbf{k}-}^{(\text{sk,o})}+\mathcal{N}_{2,\mathbf{k}-}^{(\text{sk,o})}+\mathcal{N}_{3,\mathbf{k}-}^{(\text{sk,o})},

(1.152)

with

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}_{1,\mathbf{k}-}^{(\text{sk,o})}=&-\frac{e^{2}}{128\hbar}E_{x}^{2}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{-}\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{+}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}}\frac{v^{2}}{h_{k}^{2}}\frac{\sin 2\theta}{(5+3\cos 2\theta)^{4}}\bigg\{\frac{t_{x}}{v}\sin\phi(45677+68896\cos 2\theta+20628\cos 4\theta\\
&-96\cos 6\theta+63\cos 8\theta)-\frac{t_{y}}{v}\cos\phi(16523+32240\cos 2\theta+13100\cos 4\theta-432\cos 6\theta\\
&+9\cos 8\theta)\biggr\},\end{split}

(1.153)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}_{2,\mathbf{k}-}^{(\text{sk,o})}=&-\frac{e^{2}}{16\hbar}E_{x}^{2}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{-}\frac{\partial^{2}f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{+}}{\partial(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{+})^{2}}\frac{v^{2}}{h_{k}}\frac{\sin^{2}\theta\sin 2\theta}{(5+3\cos 2\theta)^{3}}\bigg\{\frac{t_{x}}{v}\sin\phi(1819+1516\cos 2\theta+57\cos 4\theta)\\
&-\frac{t_{y}}{v}\cos\phi(477+596\cos 2\theta+15\cos 4\theta)\biggr\},\end{split}

(1.154)

\begin{split}\mathcal{N}_{3,\mathbf{k}-}^{(\text{sk,o})}=&\frac{e^{2}}{16\hbar^{2}}(n_{I}U_{0}^{2})^{2}E_{x}^{2}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{-}\left[\partial_{\mathbf{k}}^{x}(\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}v_{x\mathbf{k}}^{+})\frac{\partial f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{+}}{\partial\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}}+\hbar\tau_{\mathbf{k}}^{+}(v_{x\mathbf{k}}^{+})^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}f_{0\mathbf{k}}^{+}}{\partial(\varepsilon_{\mathbf{k}}^{+})^{2}}\right]\frac{h_{k}}{v^{4}}\sin 2\theta\\
&\times\left(\frac{t_{x}}{v}\sin\phi-\frac{t_{y}}{v}\cos\phi\right).\end{split}

(1.155)

And the remaining mixed scattering terms are readily evaluated by taking the derivatives of the linear response densities.
